# 449: Chapter 451: Dylan’s Super Evolution!

"Take to the skies! Garchomp!"

A violent, domineering dragon's roar shook the arena as a ferocious Garchomp landed heavily before Cynthia.

It was none other than Cynthia's absolute ace, the pseudo-legendary land dragon—Garchomp!

The moment Cynthia's Garchomp appeared, her fans erupted into excited cheers.

For Cynthia's supporters, just seeing Garchomp take the field was enough to fill them with an indescribable sense of security.

This was something none of her other main Pokémon could bring.

Because Garchomp was strong. Very strong. Cynthia's ultimate trump card.

As long as Garchomp hadn't fallen, Cynthia had not lost. She had proven this countless times since the day she became Sinnoh Champion.

"You're bringing out Garchomp right away? You still have two other Pokémon, don't you?"

Dylan studied the dragon for a moment, then smiled at Cynthia.

"I believe only Garchomp can defeat your Flutter Mane."

Cynthia made no attempt to hide her thoughts. That Flutter Mane was far too powerful.

To be safe, she decided to send Garchomp straight in.

"I see. Then please."

Dylan chuckled and gestured for her to continue, as she clearly wasn't finished preparing.

"Garchomp!"

Cynthia raised her right hand. The Keystone in her palm shone brilliantly.

Garchomp roared skyward, the Mega Stone embedded in its body glowing in unison.

The light of Mega Evolution flared brilliantly, transforming Garchomp's body in an instant.

Moments later, accompanied by an even wilder roar, the scythe-armed Mega Garchomp appeared in all its brutal majesty.

Beep—Garchomp (Mega Evolution), Dragon/Ground type, Mach Pokémon.Ability: Sand Force.Signature Moves: Dragon Rush, Earthquake, Dig, Dragon Claw, Poison Jab…Description: More brutal than before Mega Evolution, it slices foes apart with its scythe-like arms. (Mega Evolution).

So she went straight for Mega Evolution. But even Mega Garchomp doesn't have it easy against Flutter Mane, does it?

"How confident—choosing a battle where she's at a type disadvantage."

Leaf clicked her tongue as she gazed at the elegant, black-clad Sinnoh Champion in the arena.

She really was confident, wasn't she?

Flutter Mane had Fairy typing—it was immune to Dragon-type moves. And Misty Terrain still lingered on the field.

On the battlefield—

"Say hello to our opponent, Flutter Mane."

Dylan glanced up at the fading sunlight, then snapped his fingers.

"Moonblast."

Flutter Mane screeched, a sphere of moonlight gathering before it, then shot downward at incredible speed toward Mega Garchomp.

Moonblast—super effective against Mega Garchomp. And with its Protosynthesis boosting its Special Attack, if that attack landed, even Cynthia's Mega Garchomp would lose half its life in one strike.

But such a direct attack was easy to dodge. Still, Cynthia made no move to evade.

"Poison Jab!"

The blood-red scythe in Mega Garchomp's hand gleamed with deep violet light as it swung down on the Moonblast.

The scythe cleaved into the energy sphere, violet venom clashing wildly with moonlight.

Boom!

The Moonblast detonated, waves of energy exploding outward. Mega Garchomp staggered back under the shockwave—but not a single wound marred its body.

The raging dragon raised its head, its fierce gaze locking onto the airborne Flutter Mane.

"What terrifying brute force."

Steven couldn't help but sigh as he watched Mega Garchomp cleave through Flutter Mane's Moonblast with a non-STAB Poison Jab.

That same overwhelming strength was the very reason he had lost to Cynthia—the reason his Mega Metagross couldn't stand against her Mega Garchomp.

Not technique. Not strategy. Just raw power.

This Mega Garchomp's brute force was simply overwhelming.

"True enough, but battles aren't just about raw power." Alder shrugged.

"Sunny Day's ended, Protosynthesis is gone, and Misty Terrain should fade soon too."

Cynthia's mind was racing. The blazing sunlight had already disappeared, and the Misty Terrain wouldn't last much longer.

"Garchomp, Shadow Claw!"

Mega Garchomp charged forward, each step leaving deep imprints as its heavy strides echoed across the arena.

"Closing in for an opening, huh?" Dylan considered.

Mega Garchomp's evolution actually reduced its Speed. Of course, unlike in turn-based battles, those ten missing points weren't everything.

But the fact remained—Mega Garchomp wasn't as agile as its base form.

Flutter Mane's speed gave it the option to kite Mega Garchomp around the field, risky though it might be.

And there were Stealth Rocks on the battlefield. Flutter Mane hadn't triggered them yet only because it had barely moved since entering, mostly firing from a fixed position.

But in the time it took Dylan to think, Mega Garchomp had already lunged forward, leaping high beneath Flutter Mane.

Its twin scythes glowed with ghostly energy—Shadow Claw.

"Dodge."

Dylan had sorted his plan.

Flutter Mane shifted effortlessly to the side, avoiding the direct strike without panic.

"Garchomp!"

Cynthia wasn't surprised.

Mega Garchomp halted instantly, twisting its right arm to sweep across in a vicious slash.

Flutter Mane ducked low, avoiding again.

With a growl, Mega Garchomp slashed downward with its left scythe.

Flutter Mane floated backward, dodging once more.

Roaring in frustration, Mega Garchomp refused to relent, lunging after its opponent.

"Icy Wind!"

Flutter Mane opened its maw, exhaling a freezing gust that sprayed frost into the air.

Ice-type Icy Wind! And as a Ground/Dragon, Mega Garchomp was four times weak to Ice!

Yet Garchomp didn't even blink. With a savage swing, it cleaved the icy gale apart, the frost blasting harmlessly past on either side.

"A single force breaking all technique—this Garchomp is brutal!" Mei stuck out her tongue.

"But it still can't hit Flutter Mane. Flutter Mane's too agile." Toko shook her head.

"Not necessarily, Toko." Mei grinned mischievously.

Toko froze, then quickly realized—

"The Stealth Rocks. Cynthia's waiting for the opening."

Mega Garchomp's relentless pursuit suddenly made sense.

But wasn't it strange? If Flutter Mane couldn't deal with Mega Garchomp, why didn't Dylan just switch it out?

And then it happened.

Flutter Mane, backing away to dodge, finally stumbled into a solid mass—Stealth Rock.

Boom!

"Flutter Mane." Dylan called calmly.

The stones detonated, blasting Flutter Mane backward in pain.

But its eyes glowed with an eerie black light—unseen to the others.

Something's not right…

Cynthia frowned. It was too easy. Was Dylan… handing her the win?

He could've switched Flutter Mane out. This wasn't a Gym match.

And surely he hadn't forgotten about Stealth Rocks?

With Flutter Mane forced back, one Shadow Claw from Mega Garchomp would finish it.

Already injured by Porygon2 earlier, Flutter Mane couldn't possibly take that hit.

Dylan couldn't not know this.

"Garchomp! Shadow Claw!"

The thought flashed in Cynthia's mind, but the opening was too good. Instinctively, she gave the command.

Mega Garchomp's scythe gleamed with ghostly light, slicing down toward the staggered Flutter Mane.

At that moment, escape was impossible.

But then—

"Wait! Garchomp!"

Cynthia's eyes widened. She shouted just in time.

Mega Garchomp froze, its scythe jerking off-course, barely missing Flutter Mane's head. The Shadow Claw's wind whipped through its ghostly mane.

Flutter Mane's wicked grin stiffened. The black glow in its eyes flickered.

Seriously? That close—and you missed?

If I'd known, I would've thrown myself into it!

Flutter Mane felt like it had just lost a fortune. To drag Garchomp down with Destiny Bond would've been so satisfying!

"You held back?"

The smile on Dylan's face faltered, his brow arching.

Yes—if that Shadow Claw had struck, Flutter Mane would've gone down.

But the moment it hit the Stealth Rocks, it had already triggered Destiny Bond, ready to take Mega Garchomp with it.

"Cynthia, looks like you figured it out."

Dylan met her gaze, smiling faintly.

"You had Flutter Mane set up Destiny Bond."

Cynthia let out a quiet breath. She had realized it just in time. Against Ghost-types, Destiny Bond was never to be forgotten.

And Dylan's odd plays had tipped her off.

"As expected of you, Cynthia." Dylan chuckled, withdrawing Flutter Mane into its Poké Ball.

Before disappearing, the ghost shot Mega Garchomp a resentful glare—as if it had been robbed of a kill.

"But also a pity."

Dylan sighed softly, pulling out another Poké Ball.

"You could've taken down one of my mainstays."

Indeed—had Destiny Bond worked, Cynthia would've scored that victory.

But now… that chance was gone.

"I haven't lost yet, Dylan." Cynthia's eyes sharpened.

"Then face this one."

Smiling faintly, Dylan tossed his fourth Poké Ball.

"Gengar! To the field!"

"Jie-jie~!"

A shadow fell, twisting into the shape of a grayish-black Gengar, grinning wickedly.

But before it could posture, the plump ghost yelped, clutching its short legs and blowing on its feet.

Spikes—left by the Gastrodon earlier.

"Oh?"

Agatha's brow lifted slightly.

"That's the shiny Gastly you were asked about, right?" Lorelei adjusted her glasses, recalling.

"Hehehe~ It's grown strong, hasn't it?"

Agatha chuckled darkly as a shadow rose at her feet, her own Gengar peeking out curiously at the shiny Gengar in the arena.

That shiny Gengar—it was its child.

"Stronger than you, hmm?" Agatha rubbed her ace's head.

"Jie-jie~" her Gengar gave a goofy grin, though its blood-red eyes never left the gleaming white Gengar.

"Seems giving that child to Goodshow was the right choice after all." Agatha sighed.

Had Goodshow not personally begged, she would never have let the shiny Gastly go.

If there were no promising young Trainers, she would've raised it herself—even in her old age.

On the field—

"Gengar."

Dylan raised his hand. The Keystone shone again.

Gengar floated upward, its Mega Stone blazing with radiant light.

Mega Evolution!

The glow faded—revealing a snow-white Mega Gengar with jet-black eyes.

The reversal of colors sent a wave of gasps across the arena.

"Mega Gengar…"

Cynthia's brow furrowed as she fixed her gaze on the eerie, pale apparition.

This was Dylan's Mega Evolution.

Beep—Gengar (Mega Evolution), Ghost/Poison type, Shadow Pokémon.Ability: Shadow Tag.Signature Moves: Destiny Bond, Perish Song, Curse, Hypnosis, Dream Eater, Toxic…Description: Its bonds are twisted. It is said Gengar only shows interest in prey it intends to hunt. (Mega Evolution).

With refined grace, Dylan clasped his Keystone, smiling elegantly at Cynthia.

"Now then, Cynthia…"

"Shall we have a delightful duel of Mega Evolutions?"

(End of Chapter)

---

# 450: Chapter 452: The Vicious Champion

"Let's have a delightful duel between Mega Evolutions!"

Dylan was brimming with excitement. Cynthia's Mega Garchomp was her ultimate trump card, her most iconic partner.

Only by defeating Mega Garchomp could one truly claim to have defeated Cynthia!

And his Mega Gengar? That was also his ace… or at least, the ace of his Ghost-type team. Of course, that's not counting his legendaries.

"Whoa~ now this is going to be good—a clash between Mega Evolutions."

Alder's eyes lit up in the viewing room. He was really looking forward to this fight.

These days, he enjoyed watching others battle more and more.

"Mega Gengar… if I remember right, Dylan doesn't use Mega Evolution very often."

Leaf crossed her right leg over her left, her fair calf swinging lazily as she spoke with interest.

"It's not just Mega Evolution," Bruno rumbled. "Dylan rarely uses any form of boosting at all."

"Even without legendaries, his strength is undeniable. And even in Gym battles, he almost never bothered with power-ups."

"Most of the time, he's a Gym Leader receiving challengers. If the challenger doesn't use power-ups, he can't exactly go first himself."

Bruno paid close attention to Dylan. After all, Dylan was the strongest Trainer in the world.

And Bruno was preparing for his second attempt at the Rota Gym.

"Oh? That's true. And Bruno, you've actually earned the Rota Badge before, haven't you?" Leaf shot him a look.

"Haha~ Dylan had faith in me." Bruno gave a wide, toothy grin, though the pride in his voice was impossible to hide.

After all, he had been acknowledged by Dylan himself!

"I'd like to challenge Dylan too, someday." Leaf rested her cheek in her pale hand, the corners of her lips curving upward.

Well—she was one of Johto's Elite Four now, an official top member of the League. Going to challenge Dylan hardly seemed out of place.

"I've already mastered Mega Evolution. I wonder if I could get Dylan to use his as well?"

She had found a Houndoomite and learned to Mega Evolve her Houndoom. Her strength had grown by leaps and bounds because of it.

"Hmph. Dylan doesn't need Mega Evolution to deal with you." Lorelei pushed up her glasses, scoffing.

"Same to you, Lorelei. Still haven't found a Laprasite?" Leaf shot her a sidelong glance, smiling sweetly.

Lorelei huffed coldly, visibly annoyed. She had been searching for a Laprasite or Dewgongite for ages, but without success.

Damn it—did her team really not have a single member capable of Mega Evolution?

Lance, sitting upright with his arms crossed, was equally lost in thought. Just where was his oversized Mega Stone supposed to be?

On the battlefield—

"Since it's Gengar…"

Cynthia raised her arm with commanding force, her gaze fierce.

She took the initiative. Her Mega Garchomp was born to attack.

"Dragon Claw!"

Mega Garchomp charged forward, its scythe-like crimson blades gleaming with blue light.

The colossal land shark barreled toward Mega Gengar with overwhelming ferocity.

"Shadow Ball!"

Dylan countered instantly.

Mega Gengar's claws came together in a twisted dimension, condensing a deep orb of ghostly energy before hurling it straight at Mega Garchomp.

"Tear it apart!" Cynthia snapped.

Mega Garchomp raised its blood-red blade and slashed down with brutal force.

Boom!

The Shadow Ball exploded as it was cleaved, its violent energy shaking Mega Garchomp back several steps, forcing it to slow its charge.

"What incredible power…"

Cynthia's eyes narrowed. This Mega Gengar's Special Attack was terrifyingly strong!

Breaking through with sheer force was going to be harder than she thought.

"Gengar, Icy Wind!"

Mega Gengar's grin stretched wide and malicious. A chilling gale burst forth from its jaws, sweeping toward Mega Garchomp.

It was the best it could do—like Flutter Mane, Mega Gengar couldn't learn Ice Beam or Blizzard, so it had to settle for Icy Wind.

"Dig!" Cynthia countered immediately.

Mega Garchomp burrowed beneath the battlefield in an instant.

"Fly up—Curse!" Dylan seized the chance without hesitation.

Mega Gengar shot skyward, surprisingly slipping between the Stealth Rocks unharmed, as though luck itself guided it.

Its eyes glowed, and a decayed, spectral nail materialized before it.

"Garchomp!" Cynthia's pupils contracted sharply.

Mega Garchomp erupted from the earth, lunging toward Mega Gengar.

But the spectral nail pierced into Mega Gengar's chest.

Mega Gengar let out a twisted, agonized cry—a sound that was both painful and eerily ecstatic.

The nail sank fully into its body, draining its energy.

Ghost-types paid a heavy price for using Curse.

Yet once complete, a sinister, barely visible aura shot forth, plunging straight into Mega Garchomp.

The Ghost-type Curse!

Mega Garchomp's rampaging charge faltered. A wave of searing agony ripped through its body, forcing it to tremble violently.

Damn it…

Cynthia's heart sank.

Ghost-type Curse—it was brutal. Self-destructive, yes, but brutally effective.

Once afflicted, the opponent suffered relentless, crushing damage, far more devastating than poison in the mid-game.

And Mega Gengar's Ability was Shadow Tag.

Which meant Cynthia couldn't even recall Garchomp to cleanse the status.

It was trapped—forced to keep fighting, or wait for death.

And Curse didn't even last that long. Professors had proven it ended faster than a weather condition.

If no recovery move was used, the outcome was certain.

"Toxic."

As the first wave of Curse wracked Mega Garchomp's body, Dylan's smile widened.

Mega Gengar cackled and spat a stream of purple venom toward its suffering foe.

"Garchomp! Dodge!"

Cynthia's expression darkened.

Mega Garchomp tried to leap back, gritting its teeth through the pain.

But it was too slow.

The poison splashed across its body. No matter how it shook, the venom sank straight into its skin.

"..."

Cynthia's lips pressed tight, her face paling.

Toxic.

Dark purple blotches spread across Mega Garchomp's scales.

The poison struck.

Though its first sting was weak compared to the Curse, Cynthia knew what came next.

Toxic only grew stronger with every turn.

"What incredible willpower~"

Dylan praised softly. Despite the pain, Mega Garchomp didn't flinch. It endured, resolute.

"Come now, Cynthia—it's your turn to attack."

He extended his hand toward her with a gentlemanly smile.

"..."

The hell do you mean, my turn?!

Cynthia stared at the grinning Mega Gengar, then at her land shark.

Her silence said it all.

"What's the point in even fighting anymore?"

Iris muttered from the viewing room, dumbfounded.

Curse, Toxic… what was next? Sleep? Confusion? Burn?

Good grief. If she were in Cynthia's place, she'd want to jump Dylan instead.

So this was what he meant by "a delightful duel between Mega Evolutions"?

Iris was convinced Dylan only Mega Evolved Gengar to use Shadow Tag—saving himself the effort of teaching it Mean Look.

"Dylan's scarily good at this…"

Wallace was genuinely impressed. That Mega Garchomp was terrifying, strong enough to defeat Steven's shiny Mega Metagross.

Yet Mega Gengar had so easily pushed it into a corner.

Leon and the others sat in silence, wondering—if it were them on the field, what could they possibly do?

On the battlefield—

"Garchomp, Dragon Rush!"

Cynthia drew a deep breath, suppressing her turmoil.

Mega Garchomp roared, energy swirling around it as it transformed into a rampaging dragon, hurling itself at Mega Gengar.

It was one of Cynthia's most trusted attacks.

Yet Mega Gengar only smiled wider.

"Protect." Dylan's command was almost casual.

Cynthia clenched her fists. You've got to be kidding me!

A shimmering green barrier flared up, effortlessly blocking the devastating rush.

The dragon's roar shook the air, but the shield held fast.

Exhausted from the charge, Mega Garchomp's form staggered back into view.

"Confuse Ray."

Dylan's voice rang again.

"Dylan!!!"

Cynthia's pupils dilated in fury. Are you serious?!

"Garchomp!"

Mega Garchomp slashed out desperately with glowing claws—Shadow Claw.

But Mega Gengar only grinned wider.

The ghostly light seeped into Garchomp's body.

Curse flared.

Then Toxic.

Mega Garchomp's body convulsed, wracked with unbearable torment.

And then—the Confuse Ray hit home.

The great dragon suddenly spun in circles, chasing its own tail in front of its mocking foe.

The damned Gengar even clapped for it.

From the stands, the audience gasped as one.

"What the hell is this?!"

"Was Dylan always like this?!"

"That poor Garchomp… Curse and Toxic both? Who could survive that?!"

"Heh… a vicious Gym Leader. And now, a vicious Champion."

"Don't you see? Gengar hasn't landed a single direct attack… and yet Garchomp is already nearly finished."

Awe, disbelief, grudging admiration—the crowd was stunned.

And Cynthia?

She was speechless—watching her Mega Garchomp spin in circles while Mega Gengar applauded.

(End of Chapter)
